=== SOURCE CODE (java) ===
package org.example;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public class SQLQueryBuilder {

    public static String select(String table, String columns, Map<String, String> where) {
        if (columns == null) {
            columns = "*";
        }
        return select(table, columns.split(",\\s*"), where);
    }

    public static String select(String table, String[] columns, Map<String, String> where) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("SELECT ");
        if (columns.length > 0) {
            query.append(String.join(", ", columns));
        } else {
            query.append("*");
        }
        query.append(" FROM ").append(table);

        if (where != null && !where.isEmpty()) {
            query.append(" WHERE ");
            Set<Map.Entry<String, String>> entrySet = where.entrySet();
            boolean first = true;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : entrySet) {
                if (!first) {
                    query.append(" AND ");
                }
                query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
                first = false;
            }
        }
        return query.toString();
    }

    public static String insert(String table, Map<String, String> data) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("INSERT INTO ").append(table).append(" (");
        StringBuilder values = new StringBuilder(" VALUES (");

        boolean first = true;
        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : data.entrySet()) {
            if (!first) {
                query.append(", ");
                values.append(", ");
            }
            query.append(entry.getKey());
            values.append("'").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
            first = false;
        }

        query.append(")");
        values.append(")");

        return query.append(values).toString();
    }

    public static String delete(String table, Map<String, String> where) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("DELETE FROM ").append(table);

        if (where != null && !where.isEmpty()) {
            query.append(" WHERE ");
            Set<Map.Entry<String, String>> entrySet = where.entrySet();
            boolean first = true;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : entrySet) {
                if (!first) {
                    query.append(" AND ");
                }
                query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
                first = false;
            }
        }
        return query.toString();
    }

    public static String update(String table, Map<String, String> data, Map<String, String> where) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("UPDATE ").append(table).append(" SET ");

        boolean first = true;
        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : data.entrySet()) {
            if (!first) {
                query.append(", ");
            }
            query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
            first = false;
        }

        if (where != null && !where.isEmpty()) {
            query.append(" WHERE ");
            Set<Map.Entry<String, String>> entrySet = where.entrySet();
            boolean firstWhere = true;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : entrySet) {
                if (!firstWhere) {
                    query.append(" AND ");
                }
                query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
                firstWhere = false;
            }
        }
        return query.toString();
    }
}


=== PROMPT ===
You are an expert programmer. Translate the following java code to cpp.
- Keep behavior identical, including inputs/outputs, side effects, edge cases, and exceptions.
- Use idiomatic cpp only when it does not change behavior.

```java
package org.example;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public class SQLQueryBuilder {

    public static String select(String table, String columns, Map<String, String> where) {
        if (columns == null) {
            columns = "*";
        }
        return select(table, columns.split(",\\s*"), where);
    }

    public static String select(String table, String[] columns, Map<String, String> where) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("SELECT ");
        if (columns.length > 0) {
            query.append(String.join(", ", columns));
        } else {
            query.append("*");
        }
        query.append(" FROM ").append(table);

        if (where != null && !where.isEmpty()) {
            query.append(" WHERE ");
            Set<Map.Entry<String, String>> entrySet = where.entrySet();
            boolean first = true;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : entrySet) {
                if (!first) {
                    query.append(" AND ");
                }
                query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
                first = false;
            }
        }
        return query.toString();
    }

    public static String insert(String table, Map<String, String> data) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("INSERT INTO ").append(table).append(" (");
        StringBuilder values = new StringBuilder(" VALUES (");

        boolean first = true;
        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : data.entrySet()) {
            if (!first) {
                query.append(", ");
                values.append(", ");
            }
            query.append(entry.getKey());
            values.append("'").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
            first = false;
        }

        query.append(")");
        values.append(")");

        return query.append(values).toString();
    }

    public static String delete(String table, Map<String, String> where) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("DELETE FROM ").append(table);

        if (where != null && !where.isEmpty()) {
            query.append(" WHERE ");
            Set<Map.Entry<String, String>> entrySet = where.entrySet();
            boolean first = true;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : entrySet) {
                if (!first) {
                    query.append(" AND ");
                }
                query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
                first = false;
            }
        }
        return query.toString();
    }

    public static String update(String table, Map<String, String> data, Map<String, String> where) {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder("UPDATE ").append(table).append(" SET ");

        boolean first = true;
        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : data.entrySet()) {
            if (!first) {
                query.append(", ");
            }
            query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
            first = false;
        }

        if (where != null && !where.isEmpty()) {
            query.append(" WHERE ");
            Set<Map.Entry<String, String>> entrySet = where.entrySet();
            boolean firstWhere = true;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : entrySet) {
                if (!firstWhere) {
                    query.append(" AND ");
                }
                query.append(entry.getKey()).append("='").append(entry.getValue()).append("'");
                firstWhere = false;
            }
        }
        return query.toString();
    }
}

```

- Identify only the pieces that must remain equivalent: signatures, I/O contract, stateful behavior, control flow, and key API semantics.
- Turn that mapping into target-language structure promptly.
- Use one short trap scan for the single most likely language-pair failure mode.
- If a mismatch appears, patch only the affected local section and recheck once.
- If no concrete mismatch appears, close instead of reopening the mapping repeatedly.

Output only the translated cpp code in a code block.

In C++, we can implement a split function:

```cpp
std::vector<std::string> splitColumns(const std::string& s) {
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (true) {
        size_t pos = s.find(',', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            result.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        result.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        // skip comma and following whitespace
        size_t i = pos + 1;
        while (i < s.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]))) {
            ++i;
        }
        start = i;
    }
    return result;
}
```

This matches Java's `split(",\\s*")`? Let's test: `"a , b"` -> start=0, find comma at 2, push s.substr(0,2) = "a " (since pos-start=2, chars 0,1 = 'a',' '). Then skip comma at 2, i=3, skip space at 3, i=4. start=4. Next find comma from 4, npos, push s.substr(4) = "b". Good. `"a, b"` -> find comma at 1, push "a", skip comma and space, start=3, push "b". Good. `"a,b"` -> push "a", start=2, push "b". Good. `""` -> find comma npos, push s.substr(0) = "" -> vector with one empty string. Good. `"*"` -> vector ["*"].

Now, `String.join(", ", columns)` in Java: joins array elements with ", ". In C++, we can do:

```cpp
std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep) {
    std::string result;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) result += sep;
        result += v[i];
    }
    return result;
}
```

Define `using StringMap = std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>;`

Implement `splitColumns`:

```cpp
static std::vector<std::string> splitColumns(const std::string& s) {
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (true) {
        size_t pos = s.find(',', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            result.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        result.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        size_t i = pos + 1;
        while (i < s.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]))) {
            ++i;
        }
        start = i;
    }
    return result;
}
```

Implement `join`:

```cpp
static std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep) {
    std::string result;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) result += sep;
        result += v[i];
    }
    return result;
}
```

Implement `appendWhere` helper to avoid duplication:

```cpp
static void appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where) {
    if (where && !where->empty()) {
        query += " WHERE ";
        bool first = true;
        for (const auto& entry : *where) {
            if (!first) query += " AND ";
            query += entry.first;
            query += "='";
            query += entry.second;
            query += "'";
            first = false;
        }
    }
}
```

But the Java code uses `StringBuilder` and appends. We can use `std::string` with `+=`.

```cpp
static std::string select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>& columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns.empty()) {
        query += join(columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
static std::string insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap& data) {
    std::string query = "INSERT INTO ";
    query += table;
    query += " (";
    std::string values = " VALUES (";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
            values += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        values += "'";
        values += entry.second;
        values += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    query += ")";
    values += ")";
    query += values;
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>
#include <cctype>

class SQLQueryBuilder {
public:
    using StringMap = std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>;

    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>& columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap& data);
    static std::string deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string update(const std::string& table, const StringMap& data, const StringMap* where);

private:
    static std::vector<std::string> splitColumns(const std::string& s);
    static std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep);
    static void appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where);
};
```

Now, the `select` overload with `const char* columns` and the vector overload. The Java code's `select(String table, String columns, Map<String, String> where)` calls `select(table, columns.split(",\\s*"), where)`. In our C++, the `const char*` version will call `splitColumns` and then the vector version. Good.

Let's implement `splitColumns` carefully.

```cpp
std::vector<std::string> SQLQueryBuilder::splitColumns(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.empty()) {
        return {""};
    }
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (true) {
        size_t pos = s.find(',', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            result.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        result.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        size_t i = pos + 1;
        while (i < s.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]))) {
            ++i;
        }
        start = i;
    }
    // Remove trailing empty strings, matching Java's split with limit 0.
    while (!result.empty() && result.back().empty()) {
        result.pop_back();
    }
    return result;
}
```

But wait, if `s` is empty, we return `{""}`. If `s` is not empty but after removing trailing empties the result becomes empty? Is that possible? For example, `s = ","` -> result ["", ""]; pop -> [""]; not empty. `s = ",,"` -> result ["", "", ""]; pop -> ["", ""]; not empty. `s = "a,"` -> ["a"]; not empty. So result will never become empty because the first element is the substring before the first delimiter, which could be empty but there is at least one delimiter? If `s = ","`, first element is empty, second is empty; after pop, one empty remains. If `s = ", "` (comma space), delimiter matches comma+space, first element "", second ""; after pop, [""]. So result always has at least one element. Good.

But wait, what about `s = " "` (single space)? No comma, result [" "]; not empty. Good.

Now, the `join` function:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep) {
    std::string result;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) result += sep;
        result += v[i];
    }
    return result;
}
```

Now, `appendWhere`:

```cpp
void SQLQueryBuilder::appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where) {
    if (where && !where->empty()) {
        query += " WHERE ";
        bool first = true;
        for (const auto& entry : *where) {
            if (!first) {
                query += " AND ";
            }
            query += entry.first;
            query += "='";
            query += entry.second;
            query += "'";
            first = false;
        }
    }
}
```

Now, `select` implementations:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;
    return select(table, splitColumns(cols), where);
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>& columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns.empty()) {
        query += join(columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

Wait, if `columns` is nullptr, `cols = "*"`, splitColumns("*") -> ["*"], vector version: `!columns.empty()` true, join -> "*", query = "SELECT * FROM table". Good.

But what if `columns` is a `const char*` that is an empty string `""`? `cols = ""`, splitColumns("") -> {""}, vector version: `!columns.empty()` true, join -> "", query = "SELECT  FROM table". Good.

Now, `insert`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap& data) {
    std::string query = "INSERT INTO ";
    query += table;
    query += " (";
    std::string values = " VALUES (";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
            values += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        values += "'";
        values += entry.second;
        values += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    query += ")";
    values += ")";
    query += values;
    return query;
}
```

`deleteQuery`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "DELETE FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

`update`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::update(const std::string& table, const StringMap& data, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "UPDATE ";
    query += table;
    query += " SET ";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        query += "='";
        query += entry.second;
        query += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

Now, let's think about the Java code's `select` overload with `String columns` and `Map where`. In Java, if `where` is null, it's okay. Our `select` with `const char*` takes `const StringMap* where`; if null, appendWhere does nothing. Good.

But what about the Java code's `select` overload with `String[] columns` and `Map where`? In Java, if `columns` is null, `columns.length` would throw NPE. In our vector version, if `columns` is an empty vector, it appends "*". If the caller passes a null pointer? We use reference, so cannot be null. Good.

Now, let's consider the `StringMap` type alias. Should it be `std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>` or `std::map`? I'm still uneasy. Let's write:

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>
#include <cctype>
#include <stdexcept>

class SQLQueryBuilder {
public:
    using StringMap = std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>;

    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>& columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data);
    static std::string deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string update(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data, const StringMap* where);

private:
    static std::vector<std::string> splitColumns(const std::string& s);
    static std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep);
    static void appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where);
};

std::vector<std::string> SQLQueryBuilder::splitColumns(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.empty()) {
        return {""};
    }
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (true) {
        size_t pos = s.find(',', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            result.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        result.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        size_t i = pos + 1;
        while (i < s.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]))) {
            ++i;
        }
        start = i;
    }
    while (!result.empty() && result.back().empty()) {
        result.pop_back();
    }
    return result;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep) {
    std::string result;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) result += sep;
        result += v[i];
    }
    return result;
}

void SQLQueryBuilder::appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where) {
    if (where && !where->empty()) {
        query += " WHERE ";
        bool first = true;
        for (const auto& entry : *where) {
            if (!first) {
                query += " AND ";
            }
            query += entry.first;
            query += "='";
            query += entry.second;
            query += "'";
            first = false;
        }
    }
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;
    return select(table, splitColumns(cols), where);
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>& columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns.empty()) {
        query += join(columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data) {
    if (!data) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("data is null");
    }
    std::string query = "INSERT INTO ";
    query += table;
    query += " (";
    std::string values = " VALUES (";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : *data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
            values += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        values += "'";
        values += entry.second;
        values += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    query += ")";
    values += ")";
    query += values;
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "DELETE FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::update(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!data) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("data is null");
    }
    std::string query = "UPDATE ";
    query += table;
    query += " SET ";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : *data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        query += "='";
        query += entry.second;
        query += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;

}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;

}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;

}
```

In the vector `select`:

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

```cpp
std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
```
```cpp
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
.

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>
#include <cctype>
#include <stdexcept>

class SQLQueryBuilder {
public:
    using StringMap = std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>;

    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data);
    static std::string deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string update(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data, const StringMap* where);

private:
    static std::vector<std::string> splitColumns(const std::string& s);
    static std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep);
    static void appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where);
};

std::vector<std::string> SQLQueryBuilder::splitColumns(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.empty()) {
        return {""};
    }
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (true) {
        size_t pos = s.find(',', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            result.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        result.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        size_t i = pos + 1;
        while (i < s.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]))) {
            ++i;
        }
        start = i;
    }
    while (!result.empty() && result.back().empty()) {
        result.pop_back();
    }
    return result;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep) {
    std::string result;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) result += sep;
        result += v[i];
    }
    return result;
}

void SQLQueryBuilder::appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where) {
    if (where && !where->empty()) {
        query += " WHERE ";
        bool first = true;
        for (const auto& entry : *where) {
            if (!first) {
                query += " AND ";
            }
            query += entry.first;
            query += "='";
            query += entry.second;
            query += "'";
            first = false;
        }
    }
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;
    std::vector<std::string> split = splitColumns(cols);
    return select(table, &split, where);
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data) {
    if (!data) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("data is null");
    }
    std::string query = "INSERT INTO ";
    query += table;
    query += " (";
    std::string values = " VALUES (";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : *data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
            values += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        values += "'";
        values += entry.second;
        values += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    query += ")";
    values += ")";
    query += values;
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "DELETE FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::update(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!data) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("data is null");
    }
    std::string query = "UPDATE ";
    query += table;
    query += " SET ";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : *data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        query += "='";
        query += entry.second;
        query += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
```

=== TRANSLATED CODE (cpp) ===
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>
#include <cctype>
#include <stdexcept>

class SQLQueryBuilder {
public:
    using StringMap = std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>;

    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data);
    static std::string deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where);
    static std::string update(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data, const StringMap* where);

private:
    static std::vector<std::string> splitColumns(const std::string& s);
    static std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep);
    static void appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where);
};

std::vector<std::string> SQLQueryBuilder::splitColumns(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.empty()) {
        return {""};
    }
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (true) {
        size_t pos = s.find(',', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            result.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        result.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        size_t i = pos + 1;
        while (i < s.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]))) {
            ++i;
        }
        start = i;
    }
    while (!result.empty() && result.back().empty()) {
        result.pop_back();
    }
    return result;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::join(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& sep) {
    std::string result;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) result += sep;
        result += v[i];
    }
    return result;
}

void SQLQueryBuilder::appendWhere(std::string& query, const StringMap* where) {
    if (where && !where->empty()) {
        query += " WHERE ";
        bool first = true;
        for (const auto& entry : *where) {
            if (!first) {
                query += " AND ";
            }
            query += entry.first;
            query += "='";
            query += entry.second;
            query += "'";
            first = false;
        }
    }
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const char* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string cols = (columns == nullptr) ? "*" : columns;
    std::vector<std::string> split = splitColumns(cols);
    return select(table, &split, where);
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::select(const std::string& table, const std::vector<std::string>* columns, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!columns) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("columns is null");
    }
    std::string query = "SELECT ";
    if (!columns->empty()) {
        query += join(*columns, ", ");
    } else {
        query += "*";
    }
    query += " FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::insert(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data) {
    if (!data) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("data is null");
    }
    std::string query = "INSERT INTO ";
    query += table;
    query += " (";
    std::string values = " VALUES (";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : *data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
            values += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        values += "'";
        values += entry.second;
        values += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    query += ")";
    values += ")";
    query += values;
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::deleteQuery(const std::string& table, const StringMap* where) {
    std::string query = "DELETE FROM ";
    query += table;
    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}

std::string SQLQueryBuilder::update(const std::string& table, const StringMap* data, const StringMap* where) {
    if (!data) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("data is null");
    }
    std::string query = "UPDATE ";
    query += table;
    query += " SET ";

    bool first = true;
    for (const auto& entry : *data) {
        if (!first) {
            query += ", ";
        }
        query += entry.first;
        query += "='";
        query += entry.second;
        query += "'";
        first = false;
    }

    appendWhere(query, where);
    return query;
}
